### [132] [A Material Frame: Hard Recoils from Slow Force Carriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27253)
*Francesco Serra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种修改标准模型的方式：将光子的纵向分量变为物理的、以极慢速度传播的模式（c_L << 1），这破坏了规范不变性和洛伦兹对称性。理论上该模型存在平滑的c_L→0极限，可恢复标准规范理论预言，但实验约束极其严格，暗物质探测器给出c_L ≲ 10^-60的强约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索标准模型中规范对称性和洛伦兹不变性被破坏的可能性，同时不引入新场或新能标。作者试图理解光子纵向分量物理化后的理论后果，特别是其与实验的兼容性，以及由此揭示的关于参考系、物质媒介和动量吸收的新物理视角。

Method: 通过将光子纵向分量设置为具有微小传播速度c_L的物理模式来修改标准模型。利用伽利略高阶形式对称性保护光子质量，保证理论在c_L→0时平滑恢复标准预言。随后通过分析带电粒子的切连科夫辐射过程，计算慢模式发射导致的核反冲率，并利用暗物质探测器的实验灵敏度来约束c_L的取值范围。

Result: 发现带电粒子会向这些慢模式发生切连科夫辐射，并在参考系中经历硬反冲。反冲率随c_L减小而降低，但动量转移量级约为粒子自身的动量。暗物质探测器对keV级核反冲的灵敏度给出c_L ≲ 10^-60的强约束，比通常的速度差异约束强数十个数量级。在该量级下，c_L应理解为反冲率因子而非物理上相关的速度。

Conclusion: 慢模实际上固定于空间中，使参考系表现为一种能吸收带电粒子动量的材料介质。该理论框架虽然破坏了规范与洛伦兹对称性，但在极端小的c_L值下仍与现有实验兼容，同时提供了对真空参考系物理角色的新理解。

Abstract: We modify the Standard Model by making the longitudinal component of the photon physical, propagating with small speed $c_L \ll 1$. This modification breaks gauge invariance and Lorentz symmetry, defining a preferred reference frame. No new fields or scales are introduced, and the photon mass is protected by a Galilean higher-form symmetry. The theory has a smooth $c_L\to0$ limit: the new modes decouple from standard matter and ordinary gauge theory predictions are recovered. Experimental constraints are severe. Charged particles Cherenkov-emit these slow modes, experiencing hard recoils against the preferred frame. The recoil rate decreases with $c_L$, but the momentum transfer remains of the order of the particle's momentum. Dark matter detectors, sensitive to keV-scale nuclear recoils, imply an order-of-magnitude bound $c_L \lesssim 10^{-60}$, tens of orders of magnitude stronger than speed-difference bounds. At such values, $c_L$ is better understood as a recoil-rate factor rather than a physically relevant speed. The slow modes, practically fixed in space, make the reference frame a material medium capable of absorbing momentum from charged particles.

</details>


### [133] [Suppressed Quantum Effects of Weakly Coupled Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27313)
*Yunjia Bao,Dhong Yeon Cheong,Nicholas L. Rodd,Joey Takach,Lian-Tao Wang,Kevin Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文表明探测弱耦合波（如轴子暗物质和引力辐射）的非经典效应存在两个基本障碍：一是现实探测器耦合到粗粒化的"有效模式"而非基本场模式，这会抹平非经典效应；二是所有非经典效应都被弱耦合的额外幂次抑制，使其比经典波本身更难探测。尽管原则上可通过高度压缩态等量子资源克服，但所需参数远超当前实验能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精密实验越来越多地针对弱耦合波（轴子暗物质、引力辐射），这些波通常被描述为经典场，但它们可能存在于没有经典对应物的量子态中。需要探究检测这些非经典效应的可行性，以及它们是否可以作为验证引力量子化的手段。

Method: 本文通过一般性证明指出两个障碍：(1) 现实探测器无法分辨场的基本模式，而是耦合到粗粒化的有效模式，这会抹平非经典效应；(2) 所有非经典效应（包括正交分量、数统计、纠缠和解相干）都被弱耦合的额外幂次抑制。论文以轴子腔体滗技术（haloscope）为具体例子进行显式分析。

Result: 证明了两类障碍都是普遍存在的且与模式占据数无关。非经典效应的探测原则上可通过高度压缩的探测器态等量子资源来克服，但所需参数远超当前实验能力。该结论适用于大多数超轻暗物质搜索，并排除了通过引力波观测来确立引力量子化的提议。

Conclusion: 弱耦合波的非经典效应在现实条件下基本不可探测，这不仅是实践层面的困难，而是存在原则上的一般性障碍。这排除了利用引力波观测验证引力量子化的方案，并对精密实验中检验量子光学效应提出了根本性的限制。

Abstract: Precision experiments increasingly target weakly coupled waves, including axion dark matter and gravitational radiation. Such waves are commonly described as classical fields, yet they could exist in quantum states with no classical counterpart. We exhibit two severe obstructions to detecting nonclassical effects, both independent of the mode occupancy. First, realistic detectors cannot resolve the fundamental modes of a field; instead they couple to coarse-grained "effective" modes, which often washes out nonclassical effects. Second, all nonclassical effects are suppressed by extra powers of the weak coupling, making them much harder to detect than the waves themselves. We prove this in general, and explicitly show how the suppression arises for quadrature and number statistics, entanglement, and decoherence. The suppression can in principle be overcome given suitable quantum resources, such as highly squeezed detector states, but the required parameters are far beyond current experimental capabilities. We use the axion cavity haloscope as an explicit example, although our conclusions apply to many ultralight dark matter searches, and rule out proposals to establish the quantization of gravity from observations of gravitational waves.

</details>


### [134] [The Dark Dimension meets the Axiverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27314)
*Kevin Langhoff,Maria Ramos,Mario Reig*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了暗维度场景与轴子多重宇宙（axiverse）结合后的宇宙学影响，发现KK激发的轴子塔与引力子塔可通过热注入改变暗物质的冻结产生过程，并通过塔间碎片化实现能量再分配，为暗维度暗物质规避强宇宙学约束提供了简洁方案，同时发展了一套连续近似方法来计算塔的碎片化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗维度场景是弦论中解决层级问题的重要构想，但在简单模型中暗维度暗物质往往受到严格的宇宙学约束。作者希望将暗维度与轴子多重宇宙结合，探索是否可以通过轴子塔与引力子塔的相互作用及能量再分配机制来放松这些约束，从而为暗物质和额外维度提供自洽的宇宙学图景。

Method: 首先分析轴子KK塔与SM等离子体的冻结产生（freeze-in）过程，计算芳子衰变常数f_a和再加热温度T_RH的参数空间；其次研究不同塔之间通过碎片化（fragmentation）相互转化的能量再分配机制，推导N个轴子塔存在时能量稀释因子1/N的简化结果；最后发展了一个连续（continuum）近似方法，将塔碎片化的积分计算简化为求积问题，兼顾物理洞察与数值效率。

Result: 冻结产生到引力子塔和轴子塔可以提供全部暗物质，参数范围为f_a在10^12 GeV到10^16 GeV之间，再加热温度在5 MeV到O(1) GeV之间。存在N≫1个轴子塔时，任意衰变塔向SM注入的能量被稀释1/N倍，从而规避了最简单暗维度暗物质模型面临的强宇宙学约束。连续近似方法成功将碎片化计算简化为求积。

Conclusion: 暗维度与轴子多重宇宙的结合不仅拓宽了暗物质产生的参数空间，还通过多塔间的能量稀释效应为暗维度暗物质提供了规避宇宙学约束的自然机制。连续近似方法的提出也为后续定量研究提供了高效的分析工具。

Abstract: We explore the cosmological implications of combining dark dimension scenarios with an axiverse. If gauge sectors are realized on branes, towers of Kaluza-Klein (KK) excitations of closed string axions can propagate through the dark dimension in addition to the tower of graviton excitations. This modifies cosmology in two ways. First, if any of these axion towers interact with the standard model (SM) plasma, they can significantly alter the freeze-in production of the cosmological abundance of tower states. Freeze-in to graviton and axion towers can provide all of dark matter (DM) for an axion decay constant in $10^{12} \text{ GeV }\lesssim f_a\lesssim 10^{16}\,\text{ GeV }$ and reheating temperatures $5\text{ MeV }\lesssim T_{\rm RH} \lesssim O(1)\,\text{ GeV }$. Second, different towers fragment into each other and redistribute energy; each tower's fraction of energy at late times is fixed by their interactions. If there are $N\gg1$ axion towers, the energy visibly injected into the SM by any decaying tower is diluted by a factor of $N$. This suppression offers a simple realization of how dark dimension dark matter can avoid strong cosmological constraints which rule out the simplest models. In the process of this exploration we develop a continuum approach to evaluating tower fragmentation which offers insight and aids numerical calculations by reducing the problem to quadrature.

</details>


### [135] [The Very Nearly Right Theory of Flavor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27315)
*Nima Arkani-Hamed,Carolina Figueiredo,Lawrence J. Hall,Claudio Andrea Manzari*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"九链纹理"（nine-link textures）的新的Yukawa矩阵参数化方案，该方案将味道数据与CP破坏直接联系起来，发现CP相位自然地聚集在π/8的倍数附近，并通过"Yukawa三角形"与幺正三角形的关系给出了关于CKM矩阵角度(α, β, γ)的精确预测，同时可能解决强CP问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CKM矩阵幺正三角形的角度经验上接近(π/2, π/8, 3π/8)这一简单的π分数组合，强烈暗示存在联系味道与自发CP破坏的基础理论。然而幺正三角形是Yukawa矩阵的复杂函数，难以从经验观测直接反推出底层理论，这构成了将味道观测与理论联系起来的核心挑战。

Method: 作者将十维味道数据空间参数化为"九链纹理"——具有九个非零元素和单一CP破坏相位的满秩Y_u,d矩阵。通过将该类纹理的十个参数拟合到味道数据，并利用Yukawa矩阵元素自然定义的"Yukawa三角形"与幺正三角形的对应关系进行分析。

Result: 发现CP相位密集地聚集在π/8的倍数附近；Yukawa三角形在小味道参数领头阶下与幺正三角形一致，但在次领头阶产生可计算的偏差，从而给出(α, β, γ)相对(π/2, π/8, 3π/8)的精确偏离预测；九链纹理具有稀疏性且行列式自然为实数，结合自发CP破坏有望解决强CP问题。

Conclusion: 九链纹理为味道物理与CP破坏之间的关系提供了一个简洁而预测性的框架，产生的精确角度预测可被下一代实验明确验证或排除，同时为解决强CP问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: A striking empirical observation about the CKM matrix is that the angles of the unitarity triangle $(α, β, γ)$ are very close to $(π/2, π/8, 3 π/8) $, simple fractions of $π$ that are suggestive of an underlying theory linking flavor and spontaneous CP violation. However, relating this empirical observation to an underlying theory of flavor is challenging, since the unitarity triangle is a complicated function of the Yukawa matrices. In this letter we present a simple picture for the Yukawas where this direct link is possible. We begin by parametrizing the ten-dimensional space of flavor data via "nine-link textures", full-rank $Y_{u,d}$ matrices with a total of nine non-zero entries, with a single CP violating phase. Fitting the ten parameters of all such textures to the flavor data reveals a wonderful surprise: the CP phases cluster tightly around multiples of $π/8$! This happens because the entries of $Y_{u,d}$ naturally define a "Yukawa triangle", in most cases identical to the unitarity triangle at leading order in small flavor parameters. Most interestingly, these two triangles are not the same beyond leading order, yielding precise predictions for $(α, β, γ)$ with calculable deviation from $(π/2, π/8, 3 π/8)$, which can be decisively excluded or strongly confirmed by the next generation of experimental measurements of the angles. The 9-link textures are sparse and their determinants are naturally real, which taken together with spontaneous CP violation can resolve the strong CP problem.

</details>


### [136] [The inseparable three and four tops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27323)
*Gauthier Durieux,Hesham El Faham,Rikkert Frederix,Davide Pagani,Marco Zaro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了四顶夸克产生（tttt）与三顶夸克产生（tttW）之间的简并问题，首次在完整NLO精度（包含所有QCD和电弱修正）下计算了tttW产生的完整NLO修正，并提出一种新颖的"窗口移除"（window-removal）方案来一致地处理不可分离的tttW+tttt联合过程，该联合预测的包含性截面比纯on-shell tttt高出10%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC实验中四顶夸克产生（tttt）与三顶夸克产生存在显著简并。传统的重叠移除方法在非共振计算中要么破坏规范不变性并产生幺正性违背，要么涉及动量重排的任意性，因此需要一种新的方案来获得一致的联合预测。

Method: 作者在完整NLO精度下计算tttW产生的所有QCD和电弱修正，包括b夸克辐射贡献。提出了一种新颖的window-removal（窗口移除）方案，可以在保持规范不变性和幺正性的同时，对不可分离的tttW+tttt联合过程产生一致预测。还研究了理想化的硬中央b-jet辐射veto方案。

Result: 联合tttW+tttt预测的包含性截面比纯on-shell tttt产生高出10%以上。形式上次主导的耦合阶在LO数值上重要，但其对应的次主导NLO修正总体上和微分层面上都基本抵消。因此完整NLO预测可以很好地用前三个LO耦合阶加上主导QCD NLO修正来近似。veto方案能有效抑制共振tttt图及其与非共振tttW图的干涉。

Conclusion: 在实验比较中应使用tttW+tttt的联合预测而非纯on-shell tttt预测，因为on-shell tttt分量实际上无法被孤立分离。b-jet veto可以定义一个相对纯净的tttW-like信号区域。

Abstract: In measurements of four-top-quark production ($tttt$), LHC collaborations observe a significant degeneracy with three-top-quark production. We compute the dominant three-top-production mode, namely associated production with a $W$ boson ($tttW$), at complete next-to-leading order (NLO), including all possible QCD and electroweak (EW) corrections. Beyond leading order (LO), $tttW$ production with the radiation of an additional $b$-flavoured quark contributes to the same final state as $tttt$ production with a $t \to bW$ decay. Away from the on-shell top-quark limit, the usual overlap removal of resonant contributions in the non-resonant computation either breaks gauge invariance and generates unitarity violation or involves a significant arbitrariness in the required reshuffling of momenta. To overcome these issues, we introduce a novel window-removal prescription that produces consistent predictions for the inseparable $tttW+tttt$ process, with both components described at NLO accuracy. We argue that such a joint prediction should be used in comparisons with experimental selections targeting $tttt$ production, since the on-shell $tttt$ component can not be isolated in practice. Such a joint prediction has an inclusive rate more than 10% higher than the purely on-shell $tttt$ one. We also study an idealised veto on additional hard and central $b$-jet radiation, which suppresses the contributions of resonant $tttt$ diagrams as well as their interference with non-resonant $tttW$ ones and therefore defines a relatively pure $tttW$-like signal region. Formally subleading coupling orders are numerically important at LO, while the corresponding subleading NLO corrections largely cancel both inclusively and differentially. Consequently, the complete-NLO prediction is well approximated by retaining the first three LO coupling orders together with the leading QCD NLO correction.

</details>


### [137] [Seesaw Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27325)
*Nicolás Bernal,Chee Sheng Fong,Óscar Zapata*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了一种名为"跷跷板宇宙学"（seesaw cosmology）的非标准热历史，其中暴胀子通过右手征中微子（RHN）将能量传递给标准模型。论文求解了玻尔兹曼系统，发现标准模型温度在暴胀子主导时期趋近平台值，之后在不同RHN主导时期分别按a^{-1/4}和a^{-3/8}标度演化，并探讨了该框架对暗物质产生的增强效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统研究通常假设暴胀后直接进入辐射主导的再加热阶段。然而，如果暴胀子通过参与I型跷跷板机制（负责产生轻中微子质量）的右手征中微子传递能量，就会产生不同于标准辐射主导的非标准热历史。这种非标准热历史可能对暗物质产生、重子生成等宇宙学现象有重要影响，因此值得系统研究。

Method: 论文在微扰再加热框架下，求解包含暴胀子、相对论性RHN、非相对论性RHN和标准模型辐射的玻尔兹曼方程组。关键创新在于保留了非热RHN分布产生时间的依赖性以及其从相对论性到非相对论性的过渡过程，而不是采用简化的瞬时衰变或瞬时热化的近似。

Result: 研究发现标准模型温度在暴胀子主导期间快速趋近一个平台值，之后在相对论性RHN主导和非相对论性RHN主导阶段分别按a^{-1/4}和a^{-3/8}标度因子幂次演化。暗物质直接通过暴胀子衰变产生时，其增强因子约为m_φ/(2m_N)量级；而紫外冻结（UV freeze-in）机制则表现出临界温度幂次p=12和20，可能在最终辐射主导时期之前产生显著贡献。

Conclusion: 跷跷板宇宙学建立了一个将中微子质量生成、暴胀后(BBN之前)的热历史以及暗物质产生和重子生成等宇宙学现象联系起来的统一框架，对理解非标准宇宙学热历史及其对粒子物理现象的观测后果具有重要启示。

Abstract: We study perturbative reheating in which the inflaton transfers its energy to the Standard Model through right-handed neutrinos (RHNs) responsible for light-neutrino masses via the type-I seesaw mechanism. We refer to the resulting nonstandard thermal history as $seesaw$ $cosmology$. When produced relativistically and sufficiently long lived, the RHNs generate a characteristic sequence of inflaton, relativistic-RHN, nonrelativistic-RHN, and Standard Model radiation domination. We solve the Boltzmann system while retaining the production-time dependence of the nonthermal RHN distribution and its relativistic-to-nonrelativistic transition. The Standard Model temperature rapidly approaches a plateau during inflaton domination and subsequently scales as $a^{-1/4}$ and $a^{-3/8}$ during relativistic- and nonrelativistic-RHN domination, respectively. We investigate the implications of seesaw cosmology for dark-matter production. Direct production through inflaton decays can be enhanced relative to conventional reheating by a factor of order $m_φ/(2m_N)$, while ultraviolet freeze-in exhibits the critical temperature powers $p = 12$ and $20$, leading to potentially large contributions before the final radiation-dominated era. Seesaw cosmology therefore connects neutrino-mass generation, the pre-BBN thermal history and phenomena such as dark-matter production and baryogenesis.

</details>


### [138] [Precise predictions for double Higgs production in association with a vector boson in Effective Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27334)
*Ramona Gröber,Michał Ryczkowski,Gioia Sacchi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了$pp \to Vhh$（$V=W^\pm,Z$）过程在SMEFT（维度六截断）和HEFT（手征展开领头阶）有效场论框架下的NNLO QCD预言，在$\sqrt{s}=13.6$和$14.0$ TeV下提供包含尺度、PDF和$\alpha_s$不确定性的包含性预言，结果以数值系数形式给出以支持任意EFT参数的快速评估。研究表明$W^\pm hh$过程中QCD修正与EFT依赖基本因子化，K因子近乎平坦；而$Zhh$过程因含$gg \to Zhh$环诱导贡献而对EFT系数有更强依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希格斯玻色子对伴随弱规范玻色子产生过程（$pp \to Vhh$）是探测希格斯自耦合和电弱规范玻色子与希格斯玻色子耦合的重要通道。为了在新物理有效场论框架下精确提取耦合信息，需要超越领头阶的QCD修正。此前该过程仅有NLO级或部分NNLO级计算，尚缺乏在SMEFT和HEFT两种有效场论框架下统一、完整的NNLO QCD预言。

Method: 计算基于夸克诱导产生过程的因子化方法：先将过程分解为离壳矢量玻色子的Drell-Yan产生及其后续衰变$V^* \to Vhh$；在$Zhh$道还补充了进入NNLO QCD的环诱导$gg \to Zhh$贡献。采用两种有效场论框架：SMEFT（维度六截断）和HEFT（手征展开领头阶）。结果以数值系数形式表达，便于任意EFT参数下的快速评估。

Result: 研究发现$W^\pm hh$产生的QCD修正与EFT参数依赖基本因子化，K因子随EFT系数变化近乎平坦；而$Zhh$产生因含胶子诱导的$gg \to Zhh$成分，其对EFT系数的依赖性更强。论文提供了$\sqrt{s}=13.6$和$14.0$ TeV下的包含性预言以及尺度、PDF和$\alpha_s$不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 该工作为$pp \to Vhh$过程在SMEFT和HEFT框架下提供了完整的NNLO QCD精度预言，填补了该过程的高阶QCD修正空白。$W^\pm hh$中QCD修正与EFT因子化的特性简化了理论预言的应用，而$Zhh$中更强的EFT依赖性则凸显了胶子诱导贡献在探测新物理中的重要性。

Abstract: We present next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) QCD predictions for Higgs boson pair production in association with a weak gauge boson, $pp \to Vhh$ with $V=W^\pm,Z$, in effective field theory descriptions of new physics. We consider both the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT), truncated at dimension six, and the Higgs Effective Field Theory (HEFT) at leading order in the chiral expansion. The calculation builds on the factorisation of the quark-induced production process into Drell-Yan production of an off-shell vector boson and its subsequent decay into $Vhh$, supplemented in the $Zhh$ channel by the loop-induced $gg \to Zhh$ contribution that first enters at NNLO QCD. We provide inclusive predictions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ and $14.0$ TeV, including scale, PDF, and $α_s$ uncertainties, and express the results in terms of numerical coefficients that allow fast evaluations for arbitrary EFT parameters in the considered ranges. We find that, for $W^\pm hh$ production, QCD corrections largely factorise from the EFT dependence, leading to almost flat $K$-factors. In contrast, $Zhh$ production shows a stronger dependence on the EFT coefficients because of the gluon-induced component.

</details>


### [139] [Photon-Tagged Energy Flow in Inclusive Endpoint $B$ Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27367)
*Shuai Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入了一种新的B介子衰变标记能量关联子（BTEC），用于解析端点区域$\overline B\to X_s\gamma$中直接光子贡献的能量流角结构。在领先幂次和自然共线角尺度下，推导出涉及标准硬系数、B介子形状函数及新测量的夸克喷注函数之间的因子化关系，无需引入新的领先幂次非微扰函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上对$\overline B\to X_s\gamma$的研究主要关注光子能谱，缺乏对反冲喷注内部能量流角结构的信息。解析该角结构有助于理解端点因子化的适用范围、检验领先幂次因子化以及改进信号与背景的区分能力。

Method: 定义B衰变标记能量关联子（BTEC），在领先幂次和共线角尺度$\tau\sim s\sim Q\Lambda_{\rm QCD}$下推导因子化关系，该关系由标准硬系数、B介子形状函数和新引入的测量夸克喷注函数组成。在单圈精度下计算该测量喷注函数，并验证其角积分恢复标准包容性夸克喷注函数。

Result: 在Bosch-Lange-Neubert-Paz参数集下，数值基准显示在固定角截断$\tau_c=2-4~{\rm GeV}^2$外有多达3-16%的迁移。利用独立约束的形状函数，角累积量可对领先幂次端点因子化提供闭合性检验，并对直接$O_7$幂次修正和分辨光子效应敏感。

Conclusion: BTEC提供了超越普通光子能谱的额外信息，能揭示包容性反冲喷注的角结构，有望在信号与背景能量流分布不同时改善判别能力，并为端点因子化提供新的检验工具。

Abstract: We introduce a $B$-decay tagged energy correlator (BTEC) to resolve the angular structure of energy flow in inclusive $B$ decays in the endpoint region, focusing on the direct-photon contribution to $\overline B\to X_sγ$. At leading power and at the natural collinear angular scale $τ\sim s\sim QΛ_{\rm QCD}$, we derive a factorization relation involving the standard hard coefficient and $B$-meson shape function together with a new measured quark jet function; no new leading-power nonperturbative function is introduced. We calculate the measured jet function at one-loop accuracy and verify that its angular integral reproduces the standard inclusive quark jet function. For the central Bosch--Lange--Neubert--Paz parameter set, an illustrative benchmark gives $3 - 16\%$ migration outside the fixed angular cuts $τ_c=2 - 4~{\rm GeV}^2$. With an independently constrained shape function, the angular cumulatives provide a closure test of leading-power endpoint factorization and are sensitive to direct-$O_7$ power corrections and resolved-photon effects. The BTEC thereby adds information on the angular structure of the inclusive recoil jet beyond the ordinary photon spectrum and may improve signal--background discrimination when their energy-flow profiles differ.

</details>


### [140] [Photon-calibrated event-activity bias and subcollision geometry in d+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}$ = 200 GeV with PYTHIA 8 Angantyr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27572)
*Muhammad Ajaz,Haifa I. Alrebdi,Muhammad Waqas*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用PYTHIA 8.316 Angantyr模型，研究了d+Au碰撞中事件活动类选择如何将碰撞几何与伴随硬散射的软响应耦合起来。通过比较硬偏置因子、不同硬探针（直接光子、终端衰变前中性π介子、anti-kT喷注）以及不同事件活动代理，模型预测最活跃类被耗尽、边缘类被增强。通过全关联事件与Ncoll重加权参考及因子化解耦诊断的比较，本文分离了几何贡献与残余硬-软关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在小核碰撞系统中，事件活动选择会将碰撞几何与硬散射周围的软响应耦合，这种耦合可能影响硬探针的测量结果和对初始几何的理解。作者旨在通过蒙特卡洛模拟系统性地量化这种耦合效应，并区分纯几何贡献与残余硬-软关联。

Method: 使用PYTHIA 8.316 Angantyr模型模拟d+Au碰撞（√sNN=200 GeV），采用直接光子、终端衰变前中性π介子和anti-kT喷注作为硬探针。引入硬偏置因子比较硬事件进入某活动类的概率与Ncoll加权的最小偏置参考。使用Ncoll重加权参考与因子化解耦诊断分离显式几何贡献和残余硬-软关联。比较STAR-like和PHENIX-like粒子级活动代理。

Result: 模型预测光子与π介子均表现出最活跃类耗尽和边缘类增强。光子标记事件比π标记或包含HardQCD事件采样更小的平均碰撞参数和更多非衍射子碰撞。STAR-like与PHENIX-like活动代理保持类排序但给出不同类概率。几何贡献与残余硬-软关联被成功分离。

Conclusion: 硬-活动类耦合在小核碰撞中显著存在，可通过几何与残余关联的分离来理解。然而，计算停留在生成器层面，未能确定残余关联的唯一微观起源，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Event-activity selections in small nuclear collision systems couple collision geometry to the soft response accompanying a hard scattering. We examine this coupling in d+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}$ = 200 GeV with PYTHIA 8.316 Angantyr, using direct photons, terminal pre-decay neutral pions, and anti-kT jets. A hard-bias factor compares the probability for a hard event to enter an activity class with an $N^{ND}_{coll}$ -weighted minimum-bias reference. The model predicts a depleted most-active class and an enhanced peripheral class for both photons and pions. Photon-tagged events also sample a smaller mean impact parameter and more nondiffractive subcollisions than pion-tagged or inclusive HardQCD events. Comparing fully correlated Angantyr events with an Ncoll-reweighted minimum-bias reference and a factorized diagnostic separates the explicit geometric contribution from residual hard-soft correlations. STAR-like and PHENIX-like particle-level activity proxies preserve the class ordering but give different class probabilities. The calculation is generator-level and does not identify a unique microscopic origin for the residual correlations.

</details>


### [141] [Unbiased Data-Driven Determination of the Nuclear Dipole Amplitude in the Color Glass Condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27603)
*Si-Wei Dai,Haowu Duan,Long-Gang Pang,Guang-You Qin,Shu-Yi Wei,Han-Zhong Zhang,Wenbin Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个物理信息神经网络框架，将共线改进的Balitsky-Kovchegov演化方程直接嵌入训练目标，无需假设初始条件的函数形式即可从数据中提取核子色偶极子振幅。利用前向强子核修正因子和相干J/ψ光产生数据，提取了^208Pb在x0=0.01处的偶极子振幅，得到饱和标度比Q_{s0,Pb}^2/Q_{s0,p}^2=3.17，与几何标度一致，并能预测pp、pPb和Pbp碰撞中的横向动量比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶子饱和限制了小Bjorken-x区域部分子密度的增长，预期在重核中最为显著。然而，以往对核胶子偶极子振幅的定量提取依赖于参数化的初始条件，引入了不受控制的模型依赖，掩盖了真实的核效应。现有方法缺乏从数据中无偏提取核结构的能力。

Method: 采用物理信息神经网络框架，将共线改进的Balitsky-Kovchegov演化方程嵌入训练目标函数中。网络在训练过程中强制执行QCD演化和动量空间正定性约束，无需假设初始条件的函数形式。利用前向强子核修正因子和相干J/ψ光产生数据提取^208Pb偶极子振幅。

Result: 提取的^208Pb偶极子振幅在可用运动学范围内复现了测量的截面，得到饱和标度比Q_{s0,Pb}^2/Q_{s0,p}^2=3.17^{+0.17}_{-0.10}，与简单几何标度一致。提取的Pb初始条件可用McLerran-Venugopalan-type形式很好地描述，与质子形成对比。利用同一振幅预测的pp、pPb和Pbp碰撞横向动量比与LHCb测量一致。

Conclusion: 这项工作首次实现了饱和区核结构的无偏、数据驱动提取，建立了将非线性演化方程嵌入机器学习提取的一般策略，为动态约束可观测量提供了一种新的方法论范式。

Abstract: Gluon saturation limits the growth of parton densities at small Bjorken-$x$ and is expected to be most pronounced in heavy nuclei. Yet quantitative extractions of the nuclear gluon dipole amplitude have long relied on parametrized initial conditions, introducing uncontrolled model dependence that obscures genuine nuclear effects. We introduce a physics-informed neural-network framework that embeds the collinearly improved Balitsky-Kovchegov evolution equation directly into the training objective, allowing the impact-parameter-averaged dipole amplitude to be determined from data without assuming a functional form for its initial condition. Applying this framework to forward-hadron nuclear-modification-factor and coherent $J/ψ$ photoproduction data, we extract the $^{208}$Pb dipole amplitude at $x_0=0.01$ with QCD evolution and momentum-space positivity enforced throughout training. The evolved amplitude reproduces the measured cross sections across the available kinematic range and yields a saturation-scale ratio $Q_{s0,\mathrm{Pb}}^2/Q_{s0,p}^2 = 3.17^{+0.17}_{-0.10}$, consistent with simple geometric scaling. The extracted Pb initial condition is well described by a McLerran-Venugopalan-type form, in contrast to the proton, reflecting the higher color-charge density of a large nucleus. Using the same amplitude, we predict the rapidity dependence of the transverse-momentum ratio in $pp$, $p$Pb, and Pb$p$ collisions, finding agreement with recent LHCb measurements at low multiplicity without any system-dependent parameters. This work provides the first unbiased, data-driven determination of nuclear structure in the saturation regime and establishes a general strategy for embedding nonlinear evolution equations into machine-learning extractions of dynamically constrained observables.

</details>


### [142] [Magnetic dipole moments as probes of doubly-bottom molecular pentaquarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27653)
*Ulaş Özdem*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在QCD光锥求和规则框架下，利用光子分布振幅计算了双底五夸克态（自旋-宇称J^P=1/2^- 和 3/2^-）的磁偶极矩，该态被解释为BΣ_b、BΣ_b*和B*Σ_b构型下的强子分子。研究发现磁偶极矩对内禀自旋结构和夸克组分高度敏感：BΣ_b构型中轻夸克主导，BΣ_b*中重底夸克主导，而B*Σ_b中轻重夸克贡献相长叠加导致磁矩显著增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双底五夸克态作为新型强子分子候选态，其内部结构尚不明确。磁偶极矩作为电磁性质的敏感探针，可以揭示这些五夸克态的内部自旋结构和夸克组分，为实验探测提供理论预测。

Method: 采用QCD光锥求和规则（LCSR）方法，结合光子分布振幅（photon DAs），分别对BΣ_b、BΣ_b*和B*Σ_b三种分子构型下的双底五夸克态（J^P=1/2^-和3/2^-）进行磁偶极矩计算。

Result: 磁偶极矩对内禀自旋结构和夸克组分表现出强烈敏感性。在BΣ_b构型中，轻夸克扇区贡献占主导；在BΣ_b*构型中，磁矩主要由重底夸克决定；对于B*Σ_b分子态，轻、重夸克扇区贡献相长干涉，导致磁偶极矩显著增强。

Conclusion: 磁偶极矩是探测分子型双底五夸克内部结构的敏感观测量，计算结果为未来实验研究提供了可检验的理论预言。

Abstract: We investigate the magnetic dipole moments of doubly-bottom pentaquark states with spin-parities $J^P=\tfrac{1}{2}^-$ and $\tfrac{3}{2}^-$, interpreted as hadronic molecules in the $BΣ_b$, $BΣ_b^{*}$, and $B^{*}Σ_b$ configurations. The analysis is performed within the framework of QCD light-cone sum rules employing photon distribution amplitudes. Our results demonstrate a strong sensitivity of the magnetic dipole moments to the internal spin structure and quark composition of the states. In particular, the light-quark sector provides the dominant contribution in the $BΣ_b$ configuration, while the magnetic moment of $BΣ_b^{*}$ is largely governed by the heavy bottom quark. For the $B^{*}Σ_b$ molecular state, both light and heavy sectors contribute constructively, leading to a significantly enhanced magnetic dipole moment. These findings indicate that magnetic dipole moments constitute a sensitive probe of the internal structure of molecular-type doubly-bottom pentaquarks and provide testable predictions for future experimental studies.

</details>


### [143] [Laplace-Space Analysis of $xF_3$ Including Nuclear Effects and Gegenbauer-Polynomial Parton Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28105)
*Shahin Atashbar Tehrani,Javad Sheibani,Elham Astaraki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对非单态结构函数xF3进行了NLO和NNLO级别的QCD分析，采用Gegenbauer多项式表示输入部分子分布函数（PDFs），并结合核修正因子及Laplace空间的DGLAP演化方程解析求解，最后通过Jacobi多项式展开重建结构函数。利用CCFR、NuTeV和CHORUS实验数据系统研究了重核靶上价夸克分布提取的改进效果，以及核效应和多种求和规则的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核效应对深部非弹性散射中价夸克分布提取存在显著影响，传统的PDF参数化在大运动学范围内可能缺乏足够的灵活性。作者旨在通过Gegenbauer多项式形式，更灵活且高效地描述核目标的xF3结构函数，从而改善价夸克分布的抽取精度和现象学一致性。

Method: 采用Gegenbauer多项式参数化输入PDF，引入核修正因子描述重核靶效应；在Laplace空间中解析求解DGLAP方程实现NLO和NNLO的QCD演化，随后利用Jacobi多项式展开将结构函数重建回Bjorken-x空间。对CCFR、NuTeV和CHORUS实验的xF3数据进行联合分析，并检验Gross-Llewellyn Smith、Bjorken和Adler求和规则。

Result: Gegenbauer多项式PDF形式为核xF3数据的描述提供了灵活且高效的框架，在大范围运动学区域内取得了改进的现象学一致性。同时系统评估了核修正对提取分布的敏感性及其对求和规则的影响。

Conclusion: Gegenbauer多项式PDF形式是描述核靶xF3数据的有效工具，能够提高价夸克分布提取的可信度，并在NLO与NNLO精度上为核效应的研究提供系统性视角。

Abstract: We present a next-to-leading order (NLO) and next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) QCD analysis of the non-singlet structure function $xF_3$, utilizing a Gegenbauer-polynomial representation for the input parton distribution functions (PDFs). The main objective is to assess how this flexible parameterization improves the extraction of valence quark distributions in the presence of nuclear effects. To this end, we incorporate nuclear modification factors into the input PDFs for heavy nuclear targets and solve the DGLAP evolution equations analytically in Laplace space. The structure function in Bjorken-$x$ space is then reconstructed using a Jacobi polynomial expansion. This combined framework enables a systematic investigation of the $xF_3$ data from the CCFR, NuTeV, and CHORUS experiments at both NLO and NNLO accuracies. We further examine the sensitivity of the extracted distributions to nuclear corrections and discuss their implications for the Gross--Llewellyn Smith, Bjorken, and Adler sum rules. Our results demonstrate that the Gegenbauer-polynomial PDF formalism provides a flexible and efficient framework for describing nuclear $xF_3$ data, yielding improved phenomenological consistency across a wide kinematic range.

</details>


### [144] [Roles of $a_0(980)^+ f_0(500,980)$ and $a_1(1260)^+η$ production mechanisms in decay $D_s^+ \to π^+ π^0 π^0 η$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28169)
*JUn He*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于BESIII实验数据，在手征幺正方法的理论框架下研究了D_s^+ → π^+π^0π^0η的衰变机制，包含两个动力学产生的共振机制（D_s^+ → a_0(980)^+ f_0(500,980)和一个中间态共振机制D_s^+ → a_1(1260)^+η），并通过不变质量谱的计算解释了a_0(980)^+的cusp效应产生、f_0(980)和f_0(500)的伴随产生以及a_1(1260)^+的衰变贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于BESIII实验数据，理论上研究D_s^+ → π^+π^0π^0η这一多体衰变过程中所涉及的动力学产生共振（如a_0(980)^+、f_0(500)、f_0(980)、a_1(1260)^+）的衰变机制，以理解这些标量/轴矢共振的产生和相互作用细节。

Method: 采用手征幺正方法（chiral unitary approach），考虑两种产生机制：(1) D_s^+衰变为两个动力学生成的共振a_0(980)^+f_0(500,980)，其中a_0(980)^+通过π^+η和K^+K̅^0耦合道产生，f_0(500)和f_0(980)通过π^+π^-、π^0π^0、K^+K^-、K^0K̅^0、ηη和π^0η等多耦合道产生；(2) D_s^+ → a_1(1260)^+η，a_1(1260)通过πρ和K̅*K−K*K̅通道产生。基于这些两体相互作用计算衰变振幅并得到各不变质量谱。

Result: 计算结果较好地重现了BESIII的D_s^+ → π^+π^0π^0η数据：(1) π^+η不变质量谱中约1000 MeV处的峰可理解为a_0(980)^+由cusp效应产生；(2) π^0π^0谱中约1000 MeV处的小结构由与f_0(500)伴随产生的f_0(980)解释；(3) a_1(1260)^+通过衰变到ρ^+π^0再经ρ^+级联衰变，能很好描述π^+π^0π^0和π^+π^0的质量谱；(4) 在π^+η谱中额外引入约800 MeV的共振仅带来边际性的拟合改善。

Conclusion: 手征幺正方法能够同时解释D_s^+ → π^+π^0π^0η衰变中的多个动力学产生共振信号，确认了a_0(980)^+的cusp起源、f_0(980)与f_0(500)的伴随生成机制以及a_1(1260)^+对高能区谱形的关键贡献。

Abstract: In this work, we theoretically investigate the decay mechanism of $D_s^+ \to π^+ π^0 π^0 η$ based on BESIII data, considering two mechanisms: the production of two dynamically generated resonances, $D_s^+ \to a_0(980)^+ f_0(500,980)$, and a process with one dynamically generated resonance, $D_s^+ \to a_1(1260)^+η$. In the first mechanism, the interactions $π^+η$ and $K^+\bar{K}^0$ are included to generate the $a_0(980)^+$, while coupled-channel interactions involving $π^+π^-$, $π^0π^0$, $K^+K^-$, $K^0\bar{K}^0$, $ηη$, and $π^0η$ are adopted to generate the $f_0(500)$ as well as the $f_0(980)$. For the second mechanism, the $πρ$ and $\bar{K}^*{K} - K^*\bar{K}$ channels are included to generate the $a_1(1260)$. With these interactions, the amplitudes are calculated in the chiral unitary approach and used to compute the invariant mass spectra of $D_s^+ \to π^+ π^0 π^0 η$. The results suggest that the peak near 1000~\text{MeV} in the $π^+η$ invariant mass spectrum can be well interpreted as the $a_0(980)^+$ arising from a cusp effect. The $f_0(980)$, generated together with the $f_0(500)$, is responsible for the small structure near 1000~\text{MeV} in the $π^0π^0$ spectrum. The $a_1(1260)^+$ and its decay to $ρ^+π^0$ followed by the subsequent $ρ^+$ decay provide a good description of the $π^+π^0π^0$ and $π^+π^0$ mass spectra. The inclusion of an additional resonance near 800 MeV in the $π^+η$ spectrum leads to only a marginal improvement of the fit.

</details>


### [145] [Charge-exchange reactions with pion and kaon beams in the NA64h experiment at CERN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28299)
*Sergei N. Gninenko,Sergey Kuleshov,Valery E. Lyubovitskij,Alexey S. Zhevlakov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论了在CERN的NA64h实验中，通过π和K介子束流在原子核靶上发生电荷交换（CEX）反应产生轻中性介子（π⁰, η, η', K̄⁰），从而搜索与夸克耦合的暗扇区粒子（通过介子的不可见衰变和振荡）。精确计算CEX截面是本工作的核心，以评估实验投影灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索与夸克耦合的暗扇区粒子是超越标准模型的重要方向。通过轻中性介子的不可见衰变和振荡来探测暗粒子需要精确的介子产额评估，而CEX截面是决定实验灵敏度的关键输入。该工作旨在为NA64h实验（动量范围5-50 GeV）提供精确的理论预测。

Method: 通过分析π⁻(K⁻) + (A,Z) → π⁰, η, η' (K̄⁰) + (A,Z-1) 的电荷交换反应过程，针对宽泛的能量范围和多种靶核进行系统的CEX截面精确计算。这包括对不同束流动量和靶核质量数的系统性评估，以获得可靠的介子产额估计。

Result: 给出了宽能量范围（5-50 GeV）和多种靶核下CEX截面的精确计算值，这些结果可用于NA64h实验的投影灵敏度评估，并可为其他相关实验提供参考。

Conclusion: 精确的CEX截面计算是NA64h实验中搜索与夸克耦合暗扇区粒子的关键基础，本研究提供的系统性截面数据将为该实验及其他相关实验提供重要的理论输入。

Abstract: We discuss a new approach to search for dark sector coupled to quarks in the invisible decays and oscillations of light neutral mesons produced in the charge-exchange (CEX) reactions with pion and kaon beams $π^-(K^-)+ (A, Z) \to π^0, η, η' (\bar K^0) + (A,Z-1)$ at nuclei target $(A,Z)$ in the NA64h experiment at CERN for $P_{\rm Lab}$ running in the interval from 5 to 50 GeV. For estimating of the projection sensitivity for the proposed searches the knowledge of the meson yield is crucial. This work is dedicated to the accurate evaluation of CEX cross sections for the wide range of energies and target nuclei which could be also useful for other experiments.

</details>


### [146] [Systematic derivation of the Boltzmann equation for electroweak baryogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28321)
*Motoi Endo,Yushi Mura,Tenta Tsuji*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在场论框架下推导了电弱重子生成的玻尔兹曼方程。作者通过逐阶处理CP破坏畴壁背景的梯度效应，代数求解了Kadanoff-Baym方程，并提取了描述准粒子态的在壳解，进而推导了味道对角准粒子模的玻尔兹曼方程，同时忽略了描述量子相干性的非对角分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电弱重子生成是解释宇宙重子不对称性的重要机制，而此前场论框架下的推导未能完整保留自能修正，且对多味道系统缺乏系统处理方法。本文旨在提供一种更一般化、更系统的推导框架。

Method: 作者首先在Kadanoff-Baym方程中逐阶纳入CP破坏畴壁背景梯度的效应并进行代数求解；随后引入电弱重子生成场景下的合理近似和假设，识别描述等离体中准粒子态的在壳分量；最后利用这些在壳解推导味道对角准粒子模的玻尔兹曼方程，并忽略描述不同味道模之间量子相干性的非对角分量。

Result: 推导结果推广了以往的场论处理，保留了自能修正效应；当忽略这些修正时，所得结果与已知的玻尔兹曼方程一致。该方法适用于多味道玻色子和费米子系统，提供了系统且透明的推导途径。

Conclusion: 该框架为电弱重子生成提供了一个保留自能修正的、系统且透明的玻尔兹曼方程推导方法，弥补了以往场论处理的不足，并适用于多味道玻色子和费米子系统。

Abstract: We derive the Boltzmann equation for electroweak baryogenesis within a field-theoretical framework. We first algebraically solve the Kadanoff--Baym equations by incorporating the effects of gradients of the CP-violating bubble-wall background order by order. We then apply approximations and assumptions appropriate for electroweak baryogenesis and identify the on-shell parts of the solutions, which describe quasiparticle states propagating in a plasma. Using these on-shell solutions, we derive the Boltzmann equations for flavor-diagonal quasiparticle modes, while neglecting the off-diagonal components describing quantum coherence between different flavor modes. Our derivation generalizes previous field-theoretical treatments by retaining self-energy corrections, and our results are consistent with the known Boltzmann equations when these corrections are neglected. This framework provides a systematic and transparent derivation applicable to multi-flavor bosonic and fermionic systems.

</details>


### [147] [Collider Spin Tomography with Missing Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28346)
*Tengyu Ai,Qi Bi,Yuxin He,Zekun Li,Jia Liu,Xiao-Ping Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在存在不可见粒子引起的运动学模糊情况下，通过将可见测量形式化为自旋密度矩阵上的粗粒化正算子值测度（POVM），来恢复碰撞自旋层析成像的方法。研究表明信息损失由可见数据映射的零空间决定而非运动学解的数量，并发展了一种仅依赖可见数据的自洽不动点展开方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在碰撞实验中，不可见粒子（如中微子）导致的运动学模糊会使得自旋层析成像变得困难。传统观点认为多重运动学解会严重破坏自旋信息的可恢复性，但本文旨在系统性地量化这种信息损失，并开发实用的重建方法。

Method: 1) 将可见测量形式化为自旋密度矩阵上的粗粒化POVM；2) 证明信息损失由可见数据映射的零空间决定；3) 推导e^+e^-→τ^+τ^-→π^+π^-+νν̄体系中可辨识的自旋系数；4) 发展基于可见数据的自洽不动点展开（unfolding）方法，不依赖理论产生模板；5) 在标准模型和反常τ偶极矩基准下进行闭合测试。

Result: 在τ+τ-→π+π-+νν̄过程中，二重运动学模糊仅使反对称自旋关联组合C_nr-C_rn不可辨识，而微分产生率和其余14个自旋系数均可辨识。不动点展开方法能准确再现真实水平的微分产生率和所有可辨识的自旋系数，而通常的平坦平均方法会产生显著偏倚。存在非平凡零空间时，可辨识子空间结合正定性可给出并发度和CHSH参数的受控范围。

Conclusion: 缺失中微子不必破坏碰撞自旋层析成像。通过正确的理论框架（粗粒化POVM和零空间分析）以及自洽的不动点展开方法，即使在运动学模糊存在的情况下，仍能可靠地重建自旋信息，为高能对撞机中的自旋关联测量提供了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: Missing neutrinos need not destroy collider spin tomography. We formulate the visible measurement under kinematic ambiguities arising from invisible particles as a coarse-grained positive-operator-valued measure on the production spin density matrix. We show that information loss is governed by the null space of the resulting visible-data map, not by the number of kinematic solutions. In $e^+e^-\toτ^+τ^-\toπ^+π^-+ν\barν$, the twofold ambiguity leaves only the antisymmetric spin-correlation combination $C_{nr}-C_{rn}$ unidentifiable, while the differential production rate and the remaining fourteen spin coefficients are identifiable. For practical reconstruction under kinematic ambiguities, we develop a self-consistent fixed-point unfolding method using only visible data, without assuming a theoretical production template. Closure tests in Standard Model and anomalous tau-dipole benchmarks show that the method reproduces the truth-level differential production rate and all identifiable spin coefficients, whereas the usual flat average over kinematic folds gives significantly biased reconstructions. When a nontrivial null space is present, the reconstructed identifiable subspace together with positivity yields controlled ranges for concurrence and the CHSH parameter.

</details>


### [148] [Dynamical flattening of halo density cusps by Q-ball dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28517)
*Alexander Libanov,Sergey Troitsky*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出暗物质Q球（非拓扑孤立子）可作为解释星系晕尖点-核心问题的自然机制。暗物质Q球在星系中心致密区域生长，其相互作用截面随孤立子质量增加而减小，从而将Q球储存的静止质量能量转化为相对论性暗区粒子，实现自调控能量损失，使暗物质晕中央尖点变平，同时保持外晕结构基本不变，并可能贡献内晕轮廓的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷无碰撞暗物质在解释大尺度结构方面非常成功，但在小尺度星系上存在张力：核心-尖点问题（内质量亏损问题）以及固定晕质量下内旋转曲线形状和中心密度的多样性。这些观测暗示暗区可能存在额外物理影响晕的内部结构，但目前尚无普遍接受的解释。

Method: 利用一个玩具但具代表性的模型，研究相互作用的暗物质Q球。这些Q球在早期宇宙暗区中产生，在晕的致密中心区域生长，其相互作用截面随孤立子质量增加而减小，倾向于在晕中心运行，将部分存储在Q球中的静止质量能量转化为相对论性暗区粒子，从而修改内部质量密度轮廓。

Result: 这种依赖于密度、自调控的能量损失机制提供了一个动力机制来使晕尖点变平，同时对外晕部分基本没有影响，并可能对观测到的内晕轮廓差异有所贡献。

Conclusion: 相互作用的暗物质Q球能够为晕尖点变平提供自然机制，并有助于解释观测到的内晕轮廓多样性，为暗物质小尺度问题提供了一种新的暗区物理解决方案。

Abstract: Cold, collisionless dark matter successfully explains a wide range of observations, including the formation of large-scale structure. Nevertheless, tensions remain on small, galactic scales, most notably the cusp-core, or inner-mass-deficit, problem and the diversity of inner rotation-curve shapes and central densities at fixed halo mass. These observations suggest that additional dark-sector physics may affect the inner structure of halos, although no generally accepted explanation has yet emerged. Here, making use of a toy but representative model, we show that interacting dark-matter Q-balls -- non-topological solitons stabilized by a conserved charge -- can provide a natural mechanism for halo cusp flattening and may contribute to observed diversity of inner halo profiles. Produced in the early Universe in the dark sector, these Q-balls grow in the dense central regions of halos, while their interaction cross section decreases as the soliton mass increases. This process operates preferentially in halo centers, converting part of the rest-mass energy stored in massive Q-balls into relativistic dark-sector particles and thereby modifying the inner mass-density profile. The resulting density-dependent, self-regulating energy loss provides a dynamical mechanism for flattening halo cusps while leaving the outer halo largely unaffected.

</details>


### [149] [Three Texture Zeros in the Hermitian Dirac sector within Type-I Seesaw Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28549)
*Richard H. Benavides,John D. Gómez,William A. Ponce*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在I型跷跷板机制下，数值求解具有三个纹理零的厄米Dirac中微子质量矩阵的基准解，在正常序（NO）和反序（IO）两种质量排序下分别找到4个和3个可行的纹理结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在I型跷跷板机制中，Dirac质量矩阵的纹理结构对中微子质量矩阵的预言能力至关重要。此前对具有三个纹理零的Hermitian Dirac质量矩阵的数值基准解研究不足，需要系统性地扫描并验证其与实验数据的兼容性。

Method: 采用I型跷跷板机制，假设重Majorana质量矩阵为对角的，Dirac质量矩阵为厄米（Hermitian）的，对具有三个纹理零的Dirac质量矩阵进行数值求解，并以NuFIT全球分析数据在3σ范围内作为约束条件，分别对正常质量排序（NO）和反质量排序（IO）进行系统扫描。

Result: 在正常排序（NO）下，有4种可行的纹理结构能够复现中微子振荡观测量；在反排序（IO）下，有3种可行的纹理结构符合要求。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了具有三个纹理零的厄米Dirac中微子质量矩阵的数值基准解，确认了在NO和IO下分别存在4种和3种与现有中微子振荡实验数据（NuFIT 3σ范围内）兼容的纹理结构，为后续唯象学研究提供了基准参考。

Abstract: We present numerical benchmark solutions for Hermitian Dirac neutrino mass matrices with three texture zeros within the type-I seesaw mechanism. The analysis is to normal ordering (NO) and inverted (IO). The heavy Majorana mass matrix is assumed diagonal, while the Dirac mass matrix is taken Hermitian. Four viable textures in NO are shown to reproduce the neutrino oscillation observables within the $3σ$ ranges of the NuFIT global analysis, and three viable textures in IO.

</details>


### [150] [LISA Reconstruction Landscape for Metastable Cosmic Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28592)
*Satyabrata Datta,Rome Samanta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究利用LISA探测器重建亚稳态宇宙弦产生的随机引力波背景。通过贝叶斯分析（在(Gμ, κ_CS)参数空间中），结合合成LISA数据和仪器噪声及未解析天体物理前景，系统评估了参数可探测性、不确定性、相关性以及从探测到参数重建的能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚稳态宇宙弦是宇宙学早期相变（如大统一理论）的潜在探针，仅探测到引力波背景信号不够，还需要恢复弦的物理参数（张力Gμ和亚稳态尺度κ_CS），以理解弦网络的衰变机制和潜在粒子物理模型。本文旨在明确LISA在哪些条件下能从"探测"走向"参数重建"。

Method: 采用真空隧穿基准模型，其中弦网络通过零温单极对成核衰变。在贝叶斯框架下对合成LISA数据进行参数估计，使用(Gμ, κ_CS)二维参数空间，比较了完整模型与简化模型（ttanh模板），并考虑了仪器噪声、未解析天体物理前景及银河系前景建模的不确定性。

Result: 重建质量由LISA频带内的寿命依赖频谱特征决定。当LISA能采样从红外尾部到高频平台间的过渡区时，数据同时包含振幅和形状信息，可恢复弦张力和亚稳态尺度；后验分布即使存在振幅-寿命折衷的相关性，仍集中在较小参数区域。无特征频谱仅能约束有限参数组合或受先验主导；高信噪比的平台状谱通过残余寿命依赖性仍可部分约束κ_CS。

Conclusion: LISA不仅能够探测亚稳态宇宙弦，还能在特定频谱特征落入其灵敏度频带时，对弦网络的寿命和物理参数进行定量重建，为区分不同宇宙弦模型及约束粒子物理理论提供可能。

Abstract: We study reconstruction of stochastic gravitational-wave backgrounds from metastable cosmic strings with the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA). In the vacuum-tunneling benchmark, the network decays via zero-temperature nucleation of monopole pairs on the string worldsheet. Initially following cosmic-string scaling, loop production is suppressed after a decay time linked to efficient loop breaking and network collapse. This finite lifetime imprints an infrared tail and a transition toward a stable-string-like high-frequency plateau. Using synthetic LISA data with instrumental noise and unresolved astrophysical foregrounds, we perform Bayesian analysis in the $(Gμ,κ_{\rm CS})$ parameter space. We map detectability, uncertainties, correlations, and localization to distinguish background detection from parameter reconstruction. Reconstruction is governed by lifetime-dependent spectral features in the LISA band. When LISA samples the transition between tail and plateau, data contain amplitude and shape information, enabling recovery of string tension and metastability scale. Posteriors may remain correlated, reflecting an amplitude--lifetime trade-off, yet occupy a small parameter region. By contrast, featureless spectra constrain only limited parameter combinations or become prior dominated. High-SNR plateau-like spectra can retain partial sensitivity to $κ_{\rm CS}$ through residual lifetime dependence even when the transition is not prominent. Finally, we assess sensitivity to Galactic foreground modeling by comparing a flexible template with a reduced tanh template. Our results show where LISA can move beyond detection to probe the lifetime of the underlying string network.

</details>


### [151] [Why 4D? Spontaneous Dimensional Selection from Gauge Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28615)
*Wen Yin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出杨-米尔斯耦合的临界性质（四维以下相关、四维边际、四维以上无关）可作为选择宏观四维时空的机制，并通过一个不预设四维非紧致时空的竞赛跑道模型，证明四维时空可通过规范临界性诱导的超对称破缺和超引力效应下的半径稳定化而自发实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何我们观测到的宏观时空是四维的，而不是其他维度数。传统的额外维度模型通常预先假设一个非紧致的四维时空，而该研究试图通过物理机制（而非人为设定）来解释四维时空的自发选择。

Method: 利用杨-米尔斯耦合在不同维度下的重整化群流性质（d<4相关、d=4边际、d>4无关），提出临界性机制。具体采用竞赛跑道（racetrack）模型进行验证，在模型中不预设四维非紧致时空，通过辐射子（radion）稳定化机制，并结合规范临界性诱导的超对称破缺与超引力效应，展示四维时空的自发实现。

Result: 在一个无需指定四维时空的受控模型中，发现四维时空确实能够通过辐射子稳定化自发实现，该稳定化由规范临界性诱导的超对称破缺和超引力效应共同驱动。

Conclusion: 杨-米尔斯耦合的临界性质提供了一个物理上自洽的维度选择机制，可作为解释宏观四维时空为何被选中的原理性证明。

Abstract: Noting that the Yang--Mills coupling is relevant below four dimensions, marginal in four dimensions, and irrelevant above four dimensions, I propose that this criticality can lead to the selection of four macroscopic dimensions. As a proof of principle, I present a concrete racetrack model of the type commonly studied in extra-dimensional scenarios, but without assuming a noncompact 4D spacetime. I find, within a well-controlled model without specifying the 4D, that 4D spacetime is spontaneously realized through radion stabilization via gauge-criticality-induced supersymmetry breaking and supergravity effects.

</details>


### [152] [Improved Approximations for Collective Neutrino Oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28619)
*Matthew Riccio,A. B. Balantekin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了描述包括集体中微子振荡在内的多种系统动力学的一体和二体su(n)哈密顿量，通过分析u(n^N)代数结构并构建乘积结构，推导了算符期望值、Rényi熵和Wigner函数的通用表达式，并基于BBGKY层级截断提出了超越平均场理论、在经典计算机上具有多项式复杂度的系统化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多量子多体系统（如集体中微子振荡）由su(n)哈密顿量控制，但其精确求解面临指数级复杂度。平均场理论虽然可行但精度有限，需要发展超越平均场的系统化方法。

Method: 首先分析系统的u(n^N)代数结构并构造其乘积结构，利用该结构推导算符期望值、Rényi熵和Wigner函数的通用表达式。随后通过BBGKY（Bogoliubov-Born-Green-Kirkwood-Yvon）层级截断方法，在经典计算机上实现多项式规模的超越平均场计算方案。

Result: 建立了系统的代数分析框架，获得了期望值、Rényi熵和Wigner函数的通用解析表达式，并开发了基于BBGKY层级截断的高效数值方法，能够以多项式资源超越平均场近似。

Conclusion: 该工作为集体中微子振荡等su(n)量子多体系统提供了一套系统的超越平均场理论框架，在经典计算资源下实现了对量子关联效应的有效描述。

Abstract: A one- and two-body $\mathfrak{su}(n)$ Hamiltonian governing the dynamics of many systems, including collective neutrino oscillations, is investigated. We start by analyzing the algebraic structure($\mathfrak{u}(n^N)$), formulate a product structure of the algebra, and utilize this to construct generic expressions for operator expectation values, Rényi Entropy, and Wigner Functions. Performing BBGKY hierarchy truncation we develop a systematic methodology for going beyond the mean field with polynomial scaling on a classical computer.

</details>


### [153] [The Conformal Origin of the Lepton Flavor Mixing Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19454)
*Cora Aigle,Alex Jourjine*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用味旋理论的形式体系，以新颖方式重新推导了TM1轻子味混合矩阵分解，该分解源于无质量标准模型在电弱相变前的共形对称性。作者进一步导出了CP破坏相位与三个混合角之间的关系，并应用于实验数据，建议TM1分解中CP破坏相位取值应为(19/12)π。在此假设下，TM1的全部四个参数均可用根式表达，有助于在味旋理论框架下区分混合参数的经典值与量子修正，进而阐明质量产生机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望从味旋理论的角度，利用此前未被报道的一个涉及U_PMNS第一行元素的恒等式，以一种全新方式重新推导TM1轻子味混合矩阵分解，并探索其与标准模型共形对称性的深层联系。同时希望通过推导CP破坏相位与混合角的关系，为区分经典值和量子修正提供新的理论工具。

Method: 采用味旋理论形式体系，利用一个此前未被报道的涉及U_PMNS第一行元素的近似恒等式，重新推导TM1混合矩阵分解；通过共形对称性论证该分解的必然性；推导CP破坏相位与三个混合角的解析关系，并将此关系应用于实验数据来确定CP破坏相位的具体数值。

Result: 推导出CP破坏相位与三个混合角之间的关系；将该关系应用于实验数据后，发现TM1分解应采用(19/12)π作为CP破坏相位值；在此假设下TM1的全部四个参数都可以用根式（radicals）形式表达。

Conclusion: TM1混合矩阵分解可视为无质量标准模型共形对称性的直接结果；将CP破坏相位设为(19/12)π后，TM1全部参数可解析地用根式表达，这为区分混合参数的经典值与量子修正提供了便利，有助于进一步阐明质量产生机制。

Abstract: Using the formalism of the flavor spin theory, we re-derive the TM1 lepton flavor mixing factor decomposition in a novel way, using a previously unreported near identity involving the elements of the first row of U_PMNS. The resulting mixing matrix is a direct consequence of the conformal symmetry of the massless SM before the EW phase transition. In addition to the previously reported results, we derive a relation between the CP violating phase and the three mixing angles. Application of the relation to the experimental data data suggests the TM1 decomposition should use (19/12)Pi as the value for the CP violating phase. With this assumption all four parameters of TM1 may be expressed in radicals, which might help in the separation of the classical values of the mixing parameters from their quantum corrections in the framework of the flavor spin theories. This in turn could help in clarification of the mass generation mechanism.

</details>
